# Pass 102 — Proposals (Step B)

Codex fallback for the DRAFTER, 2026-09-08. This table follows the completed [Step A reading](PASS-102-READING.md). Every observed current node has one row and at most one rewrite. No page copy has been changed. No juror column, judgment, operator tick or application instruction is included.

CURRENT and rewritten PROPOSED cells use **JSON string literals inside code spans**. Decode the outer quotation marks to obtain the exact DOM string: case, apostrophes, leading/trailing spaces and punctuation are preserved. KEEP is a status, not replacement text. Isolated arrows and separators are independent nodes. Row IDs are unique per page and map to the DOM locator appendix; duplicate words on different nodes are intentionally separate rows.

The attestation column uses A/E/R IDs from the reading plus thread IDs. Rewrites adapt those words to the existing offer; none is a proposed quotation or testimonial. A single-post citation supports wording only, not prevalence or a predicted outcome. KEEP reasons distinguish protected content, functional wording, plain existing language and insufficient evidence. No invented thread is attached to utility labels or facts.

Prices, names, the proof line and ledgered numbers are KEEP, including their mixed factual nodes. Existing quotations, chapter titles and documentary extracts are also retained. The industry author remains unnamed. Ledger checks compare proposed meaning with the captured current string and section 3 of `docs/LESSONS_LEARNED.md`; they do not authorize new claims.

Voice measurements give word count and average/maximum sentence length. For rewrites inside an inline text fragment, length is checked with the unchanged surrounding block. Label/fragment measurements are descriptive, not complete sentences. First singular applies to Micah; direct “you” and neutral labels are allowed. Existing protected wording can exceed the length target and is marked, not silently edited. The real `copy-lint` scanner is the banned-word check; these columns are drafting records, not Astra’s verdicts. No rewrite adds an em-dash.

Only observed UI states are covered: scrolled default content at both widths, focused skip links, selected time options and the local weekday warning. No form was submitted; unobserved server-dependent states are not fabricated.

## Draft verification record

The formatted table was parsed back into strings: all 517 CURRENT cells match the Playwright capture exactly, all 517 locator references resolve to captured nodes, and every rewrite has a phrase ID and thread. The 32 rewritten rows passed the actual `scanString` export in `lib/copy-lint.ts` with zero banned-word findings. Formatting passed. No page, component, content, dependency or gate file changed.

The ledger's literal NEVER-phrase sweep covered all 119 text-source files in `app`, `content`, `lib` and `components`, including comments. The only substring matches were inside the pre-existing encoded PDF/ZIP payloads in `lib/book-pdf.ts`, `lib/chapter1-pdf.ts` and `lib/companion-zip.ts`; none was a standalone name or phrase. This is recorded as raw-match context, not described as a zero-literal-hit sweep. No gate was changed or bypassed.

## DOM traceability appendix

Locators address the parent element and its one-based index among text-node siblings (`::text(n)` is capture notation, not a CSS pseudo-element). Resolve the parent CSS selector, then count its text-node children. View/state lists show where each node was seen, not extra proposal rows.
